# Codex conversation 01a0e38d-3f8b-7ca3-a4fc-50da5ec41528

## assistant

By “old exam copy,” I meant answers from an unfinished PrepTest saved in a browser before account saving was added. You do not need to make a decision about those now. We’ll preserve them and won’t guess which student owns them.

**Admissions is locked on the live site.** I verified that a signed-in student sees a lock in the rail and gets “Not open yet” when opening the direct URL. Joshua and admins retain access. The change passed 44 focused tests, TypeScript checking, and a production build.

Your other decisions are recorded in the [Grill Me notes](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-wayfinder-grill.md): a failed exam save pauses the clock and shows the problem, and the implementation spec does not need to wait for a student prototype test. The next step is to turn those settled decisions into the student-first implementation spec.

**Next is the Progress decision.** I checked the app: student Analytics, History, and teacher Insights currently count answers differently, so we need one rule before combining them on a Progress page.

My recommendation is to use a student’s **first graded answer** for diagnostic accuracy, show repeats separately in History and practice volume, label which sources each number includes, and use the already approved 7/14/21/30-day/All filter throughout. I’ve recorded that proposal in [decision #161](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/161).

Then I’ll settle Joshua’s read-only student preview, write the implementation spec, and split it into student-first build tickets. You don’t need to revisit the old exam-record question to keep this moving.

## My request:
[$wait-what](C:\Users\Joshu\\.agents\skills\wait-what\SKILL.md) what is student prevuew abotu andwhat is read only view mean 


## assistant

**Student preview** is a proposed button on a student’s page in your Teacher Workspace. For example, you would open Lydia’s page, choose **View student experience**, and see how her Home, My Work, Review, and Progress appear to her. It helps you check what she can find and what work needs attention. This feature has **not been built yet**.

**Read-only** means you can look, but you cannot act *as Lydia*. You cannot answer a question, mark her work complete, change her flags, or send a message from her account. A clear banner would say you are previewing Lydia’s view. To assign work or add a note, you return to your normal Teacher Workspace and act as yourself.

Understood. I corrected the plan: when you view a student’s experience, **you keep full teacher and admin controls**. Changes you make to that student’s work are recorded as your actions.

You also chose that answers you give to questions stay separate. They will not change the student’s score, History, completion, or Review. I recorded this in [teacher scope decision #162](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/162). This is the rule for the redesign; the Teacher mode has not been built yet.

<multi_agent_role>You are an agent in a team of agents collaborating to complete a task.

You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents. All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.

When you provide a response in the final channel, that content is immediately delivered back to your parent agent.
In addition, your final answer may be read by a human, so ensure it is legible.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: NEW_TASK | MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>
Payload:
<payload text>
```
You may also see them addressed as to=/root/..., which indicates your identity is /root/...

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. Only set `model` or `reasoning_effort` when explicitly requested by the user, applicable `AGENTS.md` instructions, or skill instructions; when doing so, set `fork_turns` to `"none"` or a positive integer string.</multi_agent_role>

are committed to agreeing to which of the following?\\n\\nWhich of the following is the point at issue between Martinez and Howie?\\n\\n### Reading Goal\\n*   **Read each person\'s statements** (don\'t worry about finding their \\"conclusion\\" or \\"support\\")\\n*   After you\'ve read each person\'s paragraph, go back and **revisit each claim the 1st person made.**\\n*   For each claim, ask yourself if the **2nd person agreed / disagreed / we don\'t know.**\\n*   If there aren\'t any explicit claims that the 2nd person is agreeing / disagreeing with, then ask yourself whether the 1st person is assuming anything the 2nd person would agree/disagree with.\\n\\nWe want to aspire to dial our pre-phrase pretty specifically. Since we are usually locating one and only one claim or assumption in Person 1\'s statements that Person 2 (dis)agrees with, we can be very proactive about looking for answer choices that sound like that claim, and distancing ourselves from anything that doesn’t sound like that claim / assumption.\\n\\n### Correct Answer\\nOn a Disagree or Point at Issue task, the correct answer is a claim for which we can infer that one of the people would agree and the other person would disagree (they would agree to its logical contradiction).\\n\\nOn an Agree question, the correct answer is a claim for which we can infer that each person would agree.\\n\\nFrequently, the correct answer uses the phrasing of one person, to express the opinion of the other person.\\n\\nFor example, if the first person said \\"Rigatoni is the most traditional form of pasta\\" and the second person disagreed by saying \\"Hogwash. [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '   1: /**\r\n   2:  * Resume state for every OTHER drill — Smart Drill, custom type drills, and\r\n   3:  * full sections.\r\n   4:  *\r\n   5:  * Guided drills and Intro to LR practice sets already survive a closed tab, a\r\n   6:  * sign-out, or a different device (see guidedDrillSession). Nothing else did:\r\n   7:  * a student mid-way through a Smart Drill who closed the browser came back to\r\n   8:  * a fresh shuffle, and the answers they had already given were nowhere on the\r\n   9:  * screen. The attempts themselves were never lost — those are written to the\r\n  10:  * `attempts` table per question — but the run was, which reads as lost work.\r\n  11:  *\r\n  12:  * This rides the exact same store as the other two, namespaced so the three can\r\n  13:  * never collide:\r\n  14:  *\r\n  15:  *     practice set   →  pool_slug "mc-practice-1"\r\n  16:  *     guided drill   →  pool_slug "guided::<assignmentId>"\r\n  17:  *     bootcamp day   →  pool_slug "drill::bootcamp-day::<day>::<part>"\r\n  18:  *     everything else→  pool_slug "drill::<mode>::<fingerprint>"\r\n  19:  *\r\n  20:  * Failure policy, inherited: reads degrade to null (a missing resume row must\r\n  21:  * never block starting a drill), writes surface to the caller.\r\n  22:  */\r\n  23: \r\n  24: import {\r\n  25:   loadPracticeSession,\r\n  26:   savePracticeSessionSnapshot,\r\n  27:   completePracticeSession,\r\n  28:   type PracticeSessionSnapshot,\r\n  29: } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/practiceSessionStore\';\r\n  30: import type { PracticeSession } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records\';\r\n  31: \r\n  32: export const DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX = \'drill::\';\r\n  33: \r\n  34: export type DrillResumeSession = PracticeSession;\r\n  35: export type DrillResumeSnapshot = PracticeSessionSnapshot;\r\n  36: \r\n  37: export type DrillResumeIdentity = {\r\n  38:   kind: \'bootcamp-day\';\r\n  39:   day: number;\r\n  40:   part: \'homework\' | \'practice\';\r\n  41: };\r\n  42: \r\n  43: /** Short, stable, filename-safe digest of whatever identifies the run. */\r\n  44: function fingerprint(input: string): string {\r\n  45:   let h = 2166136261;\r\n  46:   for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {\r\n  47:     h ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\r\n  48:     h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);\r\n  49:   }\r\n  50:   return (h >>> 0).toString(36);\r\n  51: }\r\n  52: \r\n  53: /**\r\n  54:  * Key one run\'s resume row.\r\n  55:  *\r\n  56:  * A full section is identified by the section itself; a filtered drill by the\r\n  57:  * filters that drew it; Smart Drill by nothing but its mode, because it has a\r\n  58:  * single continuous run per student. Returns null for the surfaces that own\r\n  59:  * their own resume rows (guided, practice sets) and for anything launched from\r\n  60:  * an explicit list of qids, where the caller already knows the set.\r\n  61:  */\r\n  62: export function drillResumeKey(input: {\r\n  63:   mode: string;\r\n  64:   config?: unknown;\r\n  65:   identity?: DrillResumeIdentity;\r\n  66: }): string | null {\r\n  67:   if (input.identity?.kind === \'bootcamp-day\') {\r\n  68:     return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}bootcamp-day::${input.identity.day}::${input.identity.part}`;\r\n  69:   }\r\n  70: \r\n  71:   const mode = input.mode;\r\n  72:   if (mode === \'adaptive\') return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}adaptive`;\r\n  73: \r\n  74:   const config = (input.config ?? null) as Record<string, unknown> | null;\r\n  75: \r\n  76:   if (mode === \'full-section\') {\r\n  77:     if (!config) return null;\r\n  78:     const pt = config.pt;\r\n  79:     const section = config.section;\r\n  80:     if (pt == null || section == null) return null;\r\n  81:     return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}section::pt${pt}-s${section}`;\r\n  82:   }\r\n  83: \r\n  84:   if (mode === \'type-drill\' || mode === \'natural-drill\') {\r\n  85:     if (!config) return null;\r\n  86:     const selected = config.selectedQids as string[] | undefined;\r\n  87:     if (selected?.length) {\r\n  88:       // A pre-selected set is its own identity: the same list re-opened is the\r\n  89:       // same run, a different list is a different one.\r\n  90:       return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}${mode}::set-${fingerprint([...selected].sort().join(\',\'))}`;\r\n  91:     }\r\n  92:     const shape = JSON.stringify({\r\n  93:       qtypes: config.qtypes ?? [],\r\n  94:       difficulties: config.difficulties ?? [],\r\n  95:       pts: config.pts ?? [],\r\n  96:       sections: config.sections ?? [],\r\n  97:       qfeatures: config.qfeatures ?? [],\r\n  98:       reasoningTypes: config.reasoningTypes ?? [],\r\n  99:       searchQuery: config.searchQuery ?? \'\',\r\n 100:       questionNumber: config.questionNumber ?? null,\r\n 101:       count: config.count ?? null,\r\n 102:     });\r\n 103:     return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}${mode}::${fingerprint(shape)}`;\r\n 104:   }\r\n 105: \r\n 106:   return null;\r\n 107: }\r\n 108: \r\n 109: /** The stored run for this drill, or null when there is nothing to resume. */\r\n 110: export async function loadDrillResumeSession(\r\n 111:   userId: string,\r\n 112:   key: string | null,\r\n 113:   legacyKey?: string | null,\r\n 114: ): Promise<DrillResumeSession | null> {\r\n 115:   if (!userId || !key) return null;\r\n 116:   const current = await loadPracticeSession(userId, key);\r\n 117:   if (current || !legacyKey || legacyKey === key) return current;\r\n 118:   return loadPracticeSession(userId, legacyKey);\r\n 119: }\r\n 120: \r\n 121: /** Write where the student is right now (local-first, debounced cloud upsert). */\r\n 122: export function saveDrillResumeSnapshot(\r\n 123:   userId: string,\r\n 124:   key: string | null,\r\n 125:   snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,\r\n 126: ): Promise<void> {\r\n 127:   if (!userId || !key) return Promise.resolve();\r\n 128:   return savePracticeSessionSnapshot(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);\r\n 129: }\r\n 130: \r\n 131: /**\r\n 132:  * Mark the run finished. Nothing is deleted — the queue and every answer stay\r\n 133:  * in the row for review — it simply stops being offered as a resume, so the\r\n 134:  * next launch of the same drill draws fresh questions.\r\n 135:  */\r\n 136: export function completeDrillResumeSession(\r\n 137:   userId: string,\r\n 138:   key: string | null,\r\n 139:   snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,\r\n 140: ): Promise<void> {\r\n 141:   if (!userId || !key) return Promise.resolve();\r\n 142:   return completePracticeSession(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);\r\n 143: }\r\n 144: \r\n 145: /**\r\n 146:  * Is this stored row worth resuming?\r\n 147:  *\r\n 148:  * Unlike a guided drill, these runs are drawn fresh on every launch, so there\r\n 149:  * is no fixed set to compare against — the stored queue IS the run. It is\r\n 150:  * resumable when it is still in progress and the student actually answered\r\n 151:  * something; a row with no answers has nothing to restore and would only\r\n 152:  * freeze a stale shuffle in place.\r\n 153:  */\r\n 154: export function isResumableDrillSession(stored: DrillResumeSession | null): boolean {\r\n 155:   return (\r\n 156:     !!stored &&\r\n 157:     stored.status === \'in_progress\' &&\r\n 158:     stored.question_queue.length > 0 &&\r\n 159:     stored.attempts.length > 0\r\n 160:   );\r\n 161: }\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '   1: /**\r\n   2:  * Practice-set resume store for the Intro to LR practice drills.\r\n   3:  *\r\n   4:  * A student who starts a practice set and leaves — closes the tab, clicks away\r\n   5:  * to a lesson, signs out, comes back tomorrow on another machine — must land\r\n   6:  * back on the question they stopped on, with every answer they already gave\r\n   7:  * still marked. React state and URL params cannot do that: both die with the\r\n   8:  * page. So each practice set keeps one durable row per user in the shared\r\n   9:  * bootcamp_kv persistence (the same layer module_progress uses), written on\r\n  10:  * every answer and on every question move.\r\n  11:  *\r\n  12:  * These are standalone functions rather than a hook because the drill runner\r\n  13:  * (src/pages/Drill.tsx) lives outside the bootcamp\'s React tree — the same\r\n  14:  * reason recordPracticeCompletion.ts exists.\r\n  15:  *\r\n  16:  * Failure policy: reads degrade to null (a missing resume row must never block\r\n  17:  * starting a drill). Writes throw so the caller can surface a toast rather than\r\n  18:  * silently losing a student\'s place.\r\n  19:  */\r\n  20: \r\n  21: import { getPersistence } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/factory\';\r\n  22: import { TABLES, PracticeSession, type PracticeSessionAttempt } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records\';\r\n  23: import { now } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/ids\';\r\n  24: import {\r\n  25:   MAIN_CONCLUSION_PRACTICE_POOLS,\r\n  26:   type PracticePool,\r\n  27: } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/data/practiceDrillPools\';\r\n  28: \r\n  29: /**\r\n  30:  * Deterministic per-user, per-pool record id, so every read and write lands on\r\n  31:  * the same row without a lookup. Mirrors progressIdFor\'s FNV-1a approach but\r\n  32:  * hashes user + slug together.\r\n  33:  */\r\n  34: export function practiceSessionIdFor(userId: string, poolSlug: string): string {\r\n  35:   const input = `${userId}::${poolSlug}`;\r\n  36:   let h = 0x811c9dc5;\r\n  37:   for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {\r\n  38:     h ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\r\n  39:     h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193);\r\n  40:   }\r\n  41:   let h2 = 0x811c9dc5;\r\n  42:   for (let i = input.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\r\n  43:     h2 ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\r\n  44:     h2 = Math.imul(h2, 0x01000193);\r\n  45:   }\r\n  46:   const hex = (h >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, \'0\');\r\n  47:   const hex2 = (h2 >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, \'0\');\r\n  48:   return `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${hex}${hex2.slice(0, 4)}`;\r\n  49: }\r\n  50: \r\n  51: export interface PracticeSessionSnapshot {\r\n  52:   questionQueue: string[];\r\n  53:   currentIndex: number;\r\n  54:   attempts: PracticeSessionAttempt[];\r\n  55:   /** Section clock already spent, for the surfaces that run one. */\r\n  56:   elapsedMs?: number;\r\n  57:   /** Answers that were already down when the clock ran out, if it did. */\r\n  58:   regulationAnsweredQids?: string[];\r\n  59: }\r\n  60: \r\n  61: /**\r\n  62:  * Read the stored session for one pool. Returns null when there is nothing to\r\n  63:  * resume, when the row is corrupt, or when persistence is unavailable — all of\r\n  64:  * which mean "start fresh", never "block the student".\r\n  65: Your device copy has been kept. Reload the exam to continue.');\n    this.name = 'PracticeTestConflictError';\n  }\n}\n\n/** Stable in one browser, different on another device or browser profile. */\nexport function getPracticeTestDeviceId(): string {\n  if (memoryDeviceId) return memoryDeviceId;\n  let id: string | null = null;\n  try { id = localStorage.getItem(DEVICE_KEY); } catch { /* storage disabled */ }\n  if (id) return (memoryDeviceId = id);\n  id = globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID?.() ?? `device-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`;\n  try { localStorage.setItem(DEVICE_KEY, id); } catch { /* revision still protects writes */ }\n  return (memoryDeviceId = id);\n}\n\nasync function assertAccount(userId: string): Promise<void> {\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\n  if (error || !data.user || data.user.id !== userId) {\n    throw new Error('Your account session changed. Sign in again before continuing this exam.');\n  }\n}\n\nfunction fromRow(row: RunRow): PracticeTestRun {\n  return {\n    id: row.id,\n    userId: row.user_id,\n    revision: row.revision,\n    activeDeviceId: row.active_device_id,\n    updatedAt: row.updated_at,\n    pt: row.pt,\n    pace: row.pace as PracticeTestPaceId,\n    // Pre-migration rows have no resolved clock. Preserve the old run's\n    // fallback behavior rather than assigning an assumed accommodation.\n    sectionSeconds: row.section_seconds === null && row.pace !== 'untimed'\n      ? undefined : row.section_seconds,\n    legs: row.legs,\n    currentLeg: row.current_leg,\n    breakAfterLeg: row.break_after_leg,\n    breakTaken: row.break_taken,\n    ...(row.break_started_at ? { breakStartedAt: row.break_started_at } : {}),\n    status: row.status,\n    createdAt: row.created_at,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction toRow(run: PracticeTestRun, userId: string) {\n  const raw = practiceTestRaw(run);\n  return {\n    id: run.id,\n    user_id: userId,\n    pt: run.pt,\n    pace: run.pace,\n    section_seconds: run.sectionSeconds ?? null,\n    legs: run.legs,\n    current_leg: run.currentLeg,\n    break_after_leg: run.breakAfterLeg,\n    break_taken: run.breakTaken,\n    break_started_at: run.breakStartedAt ?? null,\n    status: run.status,\n    created_at: run.createdAt,\n    completed_at: run.status === 'complete' ? run.legs.at(-1)?.completedAt ?? new Date().toISOString() : null,\n    raw_correct: run.status === 'complete' ? raw.correct : null,\n    raw_total: run.status === 'complete' ? raw.total : null,\n    scaled_score: run.status === 'complete' ? practiceTestScaled(run) : null,\n    score_source: run.status === 'complete' ? 'estimate' : null,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction pendingKey(userId: string, id: string): string {\n  return `${PENDING_PREFIX}${userId}:${id}`;\n}\n\nfunction keepPendingCopy(run: PracticeTestRun): void {\n  if (!run.userId) return;\n  try { localStorage.setItem(pendingKey(run.userId, run.id), JSON.stringify({ run, synced: false })); } catch { /* account write may still succeed */ }\n}\n\nfunction markPendingSynced(run: PracticeTestRun): void {\n  if (!run.userId) return;\n  try { localStorage.setItem(pendingKey(run.userId, run.id), JSON.stringify({ run, synced: true })); } catch { /* account write already succeeded */ }\n}\n\n/** A failed account write remains here for deliberate recovery, never silently replayed. */\nexport function readPendingPracticeTestBackup(userId: string, id: string): PracticeTestRun | null {\n  try {\n    const raw = localStorage.getItem(pendingKey(userId, id));\n    if (!raw) return null;\n    const backup = JSON.parse(raw) as { run: PracticeTestRun; synced: boolean };\n    if (backup.synced) return null;\n    const run = backup.run;\n    return run.userId === userId && run.id === id ? run : null;\n  } catch { return null; }\n}\n\nfunction remember(run: PracticeTestRun): void {\n  // A local copy is kept after successful account writes and after failed\n  // writes. It never silently replaces the account's newer revision.\n  const previous = loadPracticeTestRun(run.id);\n  if (previous?.userId === run.userId &&\n      (previous.revision === undefined || previous.revision >= (run.revision ?? 0)) &&\n      JSON.stringify(previous) !== JSON.stringify(run)) {\n    keepPendingCopy(previous);\n  }\n  savePracticeTestRun(run);\n}\n\n/**\n * An old run without an owner cannot safely be assigned to whoever happens\n * to be signed into a shared browser today. Runs created by the account-aware\n * client carry userId and can be imported without student intervention.\n */\nexport async function importOwnedDevicePracticeTestRuns(userId: string): Promise<number> {\n  await assertAccount(userId);\n  let imported = 0;\n  for (const run of listPracticeTestRuns()) {\n    if (run.userId !== userId || run.revision !== undefined) continue;\n    const { data: existing, error: lookupError } = await table()\n      .select('*').eq('id', run.id).eq('user_id', userId).maybeSingle();\n    if (lookupError) throw lookupError;\n    if (existing) {\n      remember(fromRow(existing as unknown as RunRow));\n      continue;\n    }\n    const row = { ...toRow(run, userId), revision: 0, active_device_id: getPracticeTestDeviceId() };\n    const { data, error } = await table().insert(row).select('*').single();\n    if (error) {\n      // An insert might have succeeded while its response was lost. Keep the\n      // original device copy and let the next load resolve that case.\n      if (error.code === '23505') {\n        const { data: saved, error: reloadError } = await table()\n          .select('*').eq('id', run.id).eq('user_id', userId).maybeSingle();\n        if (reloadError) throw reloadError;\n        if (saved) {\n          remember(fromRow(saved as unknown as RunRow));\n          continue;\n        }\n      }\n      throw error;\n    }\n    remember(fromRow(data as unknown as RunRow));\n    imported += 1;\n  }\n  return imported;\n}\n\nexport async function listAccountPracticeTestRuns(userId: string): Promise<PracticeTestRun[]> {\n  await assertAccount(userId);\n  await importOwnedDevicePracticeTestRuns(userId);\n  const { data, error } = await table()\n    .select('*').eq('user_id', userId).order('created_at', { ascending: false });\n  if (error) throw error;\n  const runs = (data ?? []).map(row => fromRow(row as unknown as RunRow));\n  runs.forEach(remember);\n  return runs;\n}\n\nexport async function loadAccountPracticeTestRun(userId: string, id: string): Promise<PracticeTestRun | null> {\n  await assertAccount(userId);\n  const { data, error } = await table()\n    .select('*').eq('id', id).eq('user_id', userId).maybeSingle();\n  if (error) throw error;\n  if (data) {\n    const run = fromRow(data as unknown as RunRow);\n    remember(run);\n    return run;\n  }\n  const local = loadPracticeTestRun(id);\n  if (local?.userId === userId && local.revision === undefined) {\n    await importOwnedDevicePracticeTestRuns(userId);\n    return loadAccountPracticeTestRun(userId, id);\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** Check the current writer before a PT leg commits answers or a result. */\nexport async function isActivePracticeTestDevice(userId: string, runId: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  await assertAccount(userId);\n  const { data, error } = await table().select('active_device_id, status')\n    .eq('id', runId).eq('user_id', userId).maybeSingle();\n  if (error) throw error;\n  return data?.status === 'in-progress' && data.active_device_id === getPracticeTestDeviceId();\n}\n\nexport async function createAccountPracticeTestRun(\n  userId: string,\n  blueprint: PracticeTestBlueprint,\n  pace: PracticeTestPaceId,\n  sectionSeconds: number | null,\n): Promise<PracticeTestRun> {\n  await assertAccount(userId);\n  const run = { ...createPracticeTestRun(blueprint, pace, sectionSeconds), userId };\n  remember(run);\n  const { data, error } = await table().insert({\n    ...toRow(run, userId), revision: 0, active_device_id: getPracticeTestDeviceId(),\n  }).select('*').single();\n  if (error) throw error;\n  const saved = fromRow(data as unknown as RunRow);\n  remember(saved);\n  markPendingSynced(saved);\n  return saved;\n}\n\nexport async function saveAccountPracticeTestRun(userId: string, run: PracticeTestRun): Promise<PracticeTestRun> {\n  if (run.userId !== userId || run.revision === undefined) throw new PracticeTestConflictError();\n  const deviceId = getPracticeTestDeviceId();\n  if (run.activeDeviceId !== deviceId) throw new PracticeTestConflictError();\n  keepPendingCopy(run);\n  savePracticeTestRun(run);\n  await assertAccount(userId);\n  const row = toRow(run, userId);\n  const { id: _id, user_id: _owner, created_at: _created, ...changes } = row;\n  const { data, error } = await table()\n    .update({ ...changes, revision: run.revision + 1 })\n    .eq('id', run.id).eq('user_id', userId).eq('revision', run.revision)\n    .eq('active_device_id', deviceId).eq('status', 'in-progress')\n    .select('*').maybeSingle();\n  if (error) throw error;\n  if (!data) throw new PracticeTestConflictError();\n  const saved = fromRow(data as unknown as RunRow);\n  remember(saved);\n  markPendingSynced(saved);\n  return saved;\n}\n\nexport async function abandonAccountPracticeTestRun(userId: string, run: PracticeTestRun): Promise<PracticeTestRun> {\n  return saveAccountPracticeTestRun(userId, { ...run, status: 'abandoned' });\n}\n\n/** Called only after the student chooses to continue this existing run here. */\nexport async function continueAccountPracticeTestHere(userId: string, run: PracticeTestRun): Promise<PracticeTestRun> {\n  await assertAccount(userId);\n  if (run.userId !== userId || run.revision === undefined || run.status !== 'in-progress') {\n    throw new PracticeTestConflictError();\n  }\n  const { data, error } = await table()\n    .update({ active_device_id: getPracticeTestDeviceId(), revision: run.revision + 1 })\n    .eq('id', run.id).eq('user_id', userId).eq('revision', run.revision)\n    .eq('status', 'in-progress').select('*').maybeSingle();\n  if (error) throw error;\n  if (!data) throw new PracticeTestConflictError();\n  const saved = fromRow(data as unknown as RunRow);\n  remember(saved);\n  return saved;\n}\n"}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.4 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '-- Account-backed practice tests retain the timing chosen at creation and\n-- identify the browser currently allowed to advance the run.\nalter table public.practice_test_runs\n  add column if not exists section_seconds integer,\n  add column if not exists active_device_id text;\n\nalter table public.practice_test_runs\n  drop constraint if exists practice_test_runs_section_seconds_check;\nalter table public.practice_test_runs\n  add constraint practice_test_runs_section_seconds_check\n  check (section_seconds is null or section_seconds > 0);\n\ncreate index if not exists practice_test_runs_user_created\n  on public.practice_test_runs (user_id, created_at desc);\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '-- Assignable practice tests (access tiers, stage 7).\n--\n-- A practice test here is the full sitting of one PrepTest. Assigning PT 140 to\n-- a student opens that one sitting and nothing else: the Testing Centre lists\n-- only their assigned PrepTests unless they have the Practice tests switch.\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.practice_test_assignments (\n  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n  student_id  uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n  pt          integer NOT NULL,\n  assigned_by uuid,\n  assigned_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  unlock_at   timestamptz,\n  status      text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'assigned\'\n              CHECK (status IN (\'assigned\', \'completed\', \'archived\')),\n  UNIQUE (student_id, pt)\n);\n\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS practice_test_assignments_student_idx\n  ON public.practice_test_assignments (student_id);\n\nALTER TABLE public.practice_test_assignments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admins manage practice test assignments" ON public.practice_test_assignments;\nCREATE POLICY "Admins manage practice test assignments"\n  ON public.practice_test_assignments FOR ALL\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\n\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Students read own practice test assignments" ON public.practice_test_assignments;\nCREATE POLICY "Students read own practice test assignments"\n  ON public.practice_test_assignments FOR SELECT\n  USING (\n    student_id = auth.uid()\n    AND status <> \'archived\'\n    AND (unlock_at IS NULL OR unlock_at <= now())\n  );\n\n-- True when this user may start a sitting of this PrepTest: the Practice tests\n-- switch, an admin, or a live assignment for exactly this PT.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.can_sit_practice_test(_user uuid, _pt integer)\n RETURNS boolean\n LANGUAGE sql\n STABLE\n SECURITY DEFINER\n SET search_path TO \'public\'\nAS $function$\n  SELECT\n    public.has_role(_user, \'admin\')\n    OR EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.profiles\n      WHERE class_id = _user::text AND has_practice_access\n    )\n    OR EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.practice_test_assignments\n      WHERE student_id = _user AND pt = _pt AND status <> \'archived\'\n        AND (unlock_at IS NULL OR unlock_at <= now())\n    );\n$function$;\n\n-- The API cannot be used to start a sitting the student was not given.\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS pt_runs_owner_insert ON public.practice_test_runs;\nCREATE POLICY pt_runs_owner_insert\n  ON public.practice_test_runs FOR INSERT\n  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid() AND public.can_sit_practice_test(auth.uid(), pt));\n\n-- An assigned sitting shows on Assigned work like everything else.\nALTER TABLE public.classroom_items DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS classroom_items_type_check;\nALTER TABLE public.classroom_items ADD CONSTRAINT classroom_items_type_check\n  CHECK (type = ANY (ARRAY[\'drill\', \'homework\', \'bootcamp_module\', \'note\', \'session_recap\',\n    \'announcement\', \'upload_summary\', \'weekly_plan_task\', \'smartboard_recap\', \'activity\',\n    \'practice_test\']));\n\n-- Explicit, though the schema\'s default privileges already grant these. RLS\n-- above decides which rows each role actually sees.\nGRANT SELECT ON public.practice_test_assignments TO authenticated;\nGRANT ALL ON public.practice_test_assignments TO service_role;\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.4 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '-- Practice-test runs live in the database, and legs are tagged rather than guessed.\n--\n-- Until now a sitting existed only in one browser\'s localStorage. Clearing site\n-- data lost it, a second device could not see it, and the scorer found a leg by\n-- matching "this user, this section, finished after the leg began", which is a\n-- guess that gets less true the more a student drills.\n--\n-- This migration is the schema half of that change. It is written to converge\n-- from any starting point: every statement is guarded, so a clean replay and a\n-- re-run against the live database both end at the same schema.\n--\n-- ORDER IS LOAD-BEARING in 1a. `alter table ... add column ... default` fills\n-- every existing row with the default, so adding the default before the backfill\n-- would overwrite real `section_mode` values and real completion times. Add the\n-- columns bare, backfill from the truth already in the row, and only then attach\n-- the defaults for future inserts.\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- 1a. Normalize section_history\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nalter table public.section_history\n  add column if not exists mode text,\n  add column if not exists completed_at timestamptz,\n  add column if not exists user_id uuid references auth.users (id) on delete cascade;\n\nupdate public.section_history\n   set mode = coalesce(mode, section_mode, \'full-section\')\n where mode is null;\n\nupdate public.section_history\n   set completed_at = created_at\n where completed_at is null;\n\nalter table public.section_history alter column mode set default \'full-section\';\nalter table public.section_history alter column completed_at set default now();\nalter table public.section_history alter column section_mode drop not null;\n\n-- The INSERT policy has to be REPLACED, not supplemented. PostgreSQL ORs\n-- permissive policies together, so leaving the old class-only rule in place\n-- would keep a live bypass around the new ownership test, and the composite FK\n-- further down would be decorative. The legacy policies are dropped by role\n-- rather than by name because their names differ across environments; this\n-- drops exactly the INSERT policies (\'a\' = ALTER/append = INSERT in pg_policy)\n-- and leaves SELECT/UPDATE/DELETE untouched.\ndo $$\ndeclare\n  pol record;\nbegin\n  for pol in\n    select polname\n      from pg_policy\n     where polrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass\n       and polcmd = \'a\'\n  loop\n    execute format(\n      \'drop policy if exists %I on public.section_history\', pol.polname\n    );\n  end loop;\nend $$;\n\ncreate policy "section_history_owner_insert" on public.section_history\n  for insert with check (\n    user_id = auth.uid()\n    and class_id = public.get_user_class_id(auth.uid())\n  );\n\n-- If this returns anything other than one row, an insert path was reintroduced\n-- somewhere and the ownership guarantee is gone:\n--   select polname from pg_policy\n--    where polrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass and polcmd = \'a\';\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- 1b. The runs table\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n-- id is text, not uuid: newRunId()\'s fallback path does not produce a uuid.\ncreate table if not exists public.practice_test_runs (\n  id               text primary key,\n  user_id          uuid not null default auth.uid() references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,\n  pt               int not null,\n  pace             text not null,\n  legs             jsonb not null,\n  current_leg      int not null default 0,\n  break_after_leg  int not null,\n  break_taken      boolean not null default false,\n  break_started_at timestamptz,\n  status           text not null check (status in (\'in-progress\', \'complete\', \'abandoned\')),\n  revision         int not null default 0,\n  completed_at     timestamptz,\n  raw_correct      int,\n  raw_total        int,\n  scaled_score     int,\n  score_source     text check (score_source in (\'official-table\', \'estimate\')),\n  created_at       timestamptz not null default now(),\n  updated_at       timestamptz not null default now(),\n  -- Exists solely as the target of the composite FKs below. It is what makes\n  -- cross-user leg attribution impossible at the schema level rather than by\n  -- convention.\n  unique (id, user_id)\n);\n\ncreate index if not exists practice_test_runs_user_status\n  on public.practice_test_runs (user_id, status);\n\nalter table public.practice_test_runs enable row level security;\n\n-- No set_updated_at / handle_updated_at function exists in this project\'s\n-- migrations, so it is created here rather than reused.\ncreate or replace function public.touch_practice_test_runs()\nreturns trigger\nlanguage plpgsql\nset search_path = public\nas $$\nbegin\n  new.updated_at = now();\n  return new;\nend;\n$$;\n\ndrop trigger if exists practice_test_runs_touch on public.practice_test_runs;\ncreate trigger practice_test_runs_touch\n  before update on public.practice_test_runs\n  for each row execute function public.touch_practice_test_runs();\n\ndrop policy if exists "pt_runs_owner_select" on public.practice_test_runs;\ncreate policy "pt_runs_owner_select" on public.practice_test_runs\n  for select using (user_id = auth.uid());\n\ndrop policy if exists "pt_runs_owner_insert" on public.practice_test_runs;\ncreate policy "pt_runs_owner_insert" on public.practice_test_runs\n  for insert with check (user_id = auth.uid());\n\ndrop policy if exists "pt_runs_owner_update" on public.practice_test_runs;\ncreate policy "pt_runs_owner_update" on public.practice_test_runs\n  for update using (user_id = auth.uid()) with check (user_id = auth.uid());\n\ndrop policy if exists "pt_runs_admin_select" on public.practice_test_runs;\ncreate policy "pt_runs_admin_select" on public.practice_test_runs\n  for select using (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\n\n-- Deliberately NO delete policy, for anyone. A sitting is a record of work.\n-- Terminal-state protection beyond RLS is enforced client-side by\n-- status-filtered conditional updates; a hostile owner editing their own row\n-- can corrupt only their own history, which is acceptable here.\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- 1c. Leg attribution\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nalter table public.section_history\n  add column if not exists practice_test_run_id text,\n  add column if not exists practice_test_leg int;\n\nalter table public.rc_drill_sessions\n  add column if not exists practice_test_run_id text,\n  add column if not exists practice_test_leg int;\n\n-- `add constraint` has no IF NOT EXISTS, so each is guarded by catalog lookup.\ndo $$\nbegin\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\n                  where conname = \'section_history_pt_leg_chk\'\n                    and conrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass) then\n    alter table public.section_history\n      add constraint section_history_pt_leg_chk check (practice_test_leg >= 0);\n  end if;\n\n  -- Closes the run-id-without-leg hole in both directions.\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\n                  where conname = \'section_history_pt_pair_chk\'\n                    and conrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass) then\n    alter table public.section_history\n      add constraint section_history_pt_pair_chk\n      check ((practice_test_run_id is null) = (practice_test_leg is null));\n  end if;\n\n  -- section_history.user_id is nullable, and PostgreSQL\'s default MATCH SIMPLE\n  -- skips composite-FK validation when ANY referencing column is null. Without\n  -- this an attributed row with a null user_id sails past the FK below and\n  -- consumes a run-leg slot it does not own.\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\n                  where conname = \'section_history_attr_owner_chk\'\n                    and conrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass) then\n    alter table public.section_history\n      add constraint section_history_attr_owner_chk\n      check (practice_test_run_id is null or user_id is not null);\n  end if;\n\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\n                  where conname = \'section_history_pt_run_fk\'\n                    and conrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass) then\n    alter table public.section_history\n      add constraint section_history_pt_run_fk\n      foreign key (practice_test_run_id, user_id)\n      references public.practice_test_runs (id, user_id);\n  end if;\n\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\n                  where conname = \'rc_sessions_pt_leg_chk\'\n                    and conrelid = \'public.rc_drill_sessions\'::regclass) then\n    alter table public.rc_drill_sessions\n      add constraint rc_sessions_pt_leg_chk check (practice_test_leg >= 0);\n  end if;\n\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\n                  where conname = \'rc_sessions_pt_pair_chk\'\n                    and conrelid = \'public.rc_drill_sessions\'::regclass) then\n    alter table public.rc_drill_sessions\n      add constraint rc_sessions_pt_pair_chk\n      check ((practice_test_run_id is null) = (practice_test_leg is null));\n  end if;\n\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\n                  where conname = \'rc_sessions_pt_run_fk\'\n                    and conrelid = \'public.rc_drill_sessions\'::regclass) then\n    alter table public.rc_drill_sessions\n      add constraint rc_sessions_pt_run_fk\n      foreign key (practice_test_run_id, user_id)\n      references public.practice_test_runs (id, user_id);\n  end if;\n\n  -- rc_attempts references a session by session_id alone, while its RLS checks\n  -- only the attempt\'s own user_id, so attempts can be attached to another\n  -- user\'s session. The composite FK below closes that, and needs this target.\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\n                  where conname = \'rc_drill_sessions_id_user_key\'\n                    and conrelid = \'public.rc_drill_sessions\'::regclass) then\n    alter table public.rc_drill_sessions\n      add constraint rc_drill_sessions_id_user_key unique (id, user_id);\n  end if;\n\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\n                  where conname = \'rc_attempts_session_owner_fk\'\n                    and conrelid = \'public.rc_attempts\'::regclass) then\n    alter table public.rc_attempts\n      add constraint rc_attempts_session_owner_fk\n      foreign key (session_id, user_id)\n      references public.rc_drill_sessions (id, user_id);\n  end if;\nend $$;\n\n-- One row per leg, forever. This is what makes a retried closeout safe: a\n-- duplicate insert fails with 23505 instead of scoring the leg twice.\ncreate unique index if not exists section_history_pt_leg_once\n  on public.section_history (practice_test_run_id, practice_test_leg)\n  where practice_test_run_id is not null;\n\ncreate unique index if not exists rc_sessions_pt_leg_once\n  on public.rc_drill_sessions (practice_test_run_id, practice_test_leg)\n  where practice_test_run_id is not null;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- 1d. RC first-attempt integrity\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n-- recordAttempt is read-then-insert with no constraint, so racing writes can\n-- already have double-counted. The unique index below will not build over those\n-- duplicates, so they are resolved first.\n--\n-- DEMOTE, do not delete. A duplicate is a real attempt the student made; only\n-- its is_first_attempt flag is wrong, and the review surfaces legitimately show\n-- it as a later attempt. Ranking is by (created_at, id) and never by ctid, which\n-- is physical row location rather than chronology and would retain an arbitrary\n-- row as "first".\nwith ranked as (\n  select id,\n         row_number() over (\n           partition by session_id, question_id\n           order by created_at asc, id asc\n         ) as rn\n    from public.rc_attempts\n   where is_first_attempt\n)\nupdate public.rc_attempts a\n   set is_first_attempt = false\n  from ranked r\n where a.id = r.id\n   and r.rn > 1;\n\ncreate unique index if not exists rc_attempts_first_once\n  on public.rc_attempts (session_id, question_id)\n  where is_first_attempt;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- 1e. Stale-run cleanup (used by change 5, shipped here so one migration\n--     carries all the DDL)\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n-- exclude_ids exists for change 5. A run whose dirty retry failed still LOOKS\n-- stale, because its offline progress never landed to refresh updated_at.\n-- Abandoning it server-side would let the terminal-wins reconcile then destroy\n-- that progress, so change 5 passes those ids here. The default keeps a\n-- no-argument call valid.\ncreate or replace function public.abandon_stale_practice_tests(\n  exclude_ids text[] default \'{}\'\n)\nreturns setof text\nlanguage sql\nsecurity invoker\nset search_path = public\nas $$\n  update public.practice_test_runs\n     set status = \'abandoned\',\n         revision = revision + 1\n   where user_id = auth.uid()\n     and status = \'in-progress\'\n     and updated_at < now() - interval \'14 days\'\n     and not (id = any (coalesce(exclude_ids, \'{}\')))\n  returning id;\n$$;\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"body":"Part of #155\\n\\n## Question\\n\\nConfirm the account-backed run contract for full PrepTests and timed sections: ownership, saved position/answers/elapsed time, connection loss, explicit second-device takeover, access changes, and Overview/Attempts membership. Specify treatment of older browser-only runs that have no reliable owner. Existing full-test UI writes localStorage; the 2026-09-26 discovery records approved boundaries and a remaining Q14 offline choice.","comments":[{"id":"IC_kwDOSDNwp88AAAABXaUSjw","author":{"login":"vnjkoxpl-42905"},"authorAssociation":"OWNER","body":"2026-09-28 decision: for new account-backed timed work, if an answer or position cannot save to the account, keep a temporary device backup, pause the exam and clock, and show the save problem until saving works. Joshua did not choose a display or recovery policy for older browser-only records with no student ID after repeated explanations. Preserve those records and do not attach them to any account. This issue stays open for the remaining legacy policy and implementation checks.","createdAt":"2026-09-28T08:13:20Z","includesCreatedEdit":false,"isMinimized":false,"minimizedReason":"","reactionGroups":[],"url":"https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/160#issuecomment-5866066575","viewerDidAuthor":true},{"id":"IC_kwDOSDNwp88AAAABXa5zdg","author":{"login":"vnjkoxpl-42905"},"authorAssociation":"OWNER","body":"2026-09-28 resolution for the build spec: Every new full PrepTest and standalone timed-section run belongs to the signed-in student and saves answers, question position, elapsed time, and result to the account. In-progress and completed runs appear in Attempts; unfinished runs also appear on Practice Exams Overview. Leaving/closing the exam page pauses its clock; switching tabs while the page remains open does not. A second device must explicitly choose Continue on this device, which revokes the first device save lease. If an account save fails, retain a temporary device backup, pause the run and clock, and show a blocking save state until acknowledged sync succeeds. If access is revoked, preserve the run/result but deny resuming inaccessible questions. Older browser-only records without a trustworthy student ID remain untouched and are never auto-attached or prompted for claim. Their recovery/import is deferred outside the first release; account Attempts contains only owned runs. Existing practice_test_runs migrations are a starting point, not proof of live deployment. The implementation ticket must verify live schema/RLS and replace the current localStorage-only, silent-failure runner. This resolves the planning decision without requiring Joshua to interpret an ambiguous legacy-copy scenario.","createdAt":"2026-09-28T08:55:36Z","includesCreatedEdit":false,"isMinimized":false,"minimizedReason":"","reactionGroups":[],"url":"https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/160#issuecomment-5866681206","viewerDidAuthor":true}],"state":"CLOSED","title":"Set account-backed exam resume and legacy-run policy"}\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '\r\n/** /testing/test/:runId */\r\nexport function PracticeTestRunGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\r\n  const { runId } = useParams<{ runId: string }>();\r\n  return (\r\n    <AssignmentGate\r\n      flag="has_practice_access"\r\n      authKey={runId ? `practice-run:${runId}` : null}\r\n      authorize={async (uid) => {\r\n        if (!runId) return false;\r\n        const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n          .from("practice_test_runs" as never)\r\n          .select("pt,status")\r\n          .eq("id", runId)\r\n          .eq("user_id", uid)\r\n          .maybeSingle();\r\n        if (!error && data) {\r\n          const accountRun = data as { pt: number; status: string };\r\n          return accountRun.status === "complete" ||\r\n            hasPracticeTestAssignment(uid, accountRun.pt);\r\n        }\r\n        const localPt = ptOfRun(runId);\r\n        return localPt !== null && hasPracticeTestAssignment(uid, localPt);\r\n      }}\r\n    >\r\n      {children}\r\n    </AssignmentGate>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * What an assignment-launched drill run is authorised by, if anything. Pure so\r\n * it can be tested without a router.\r\n */\r\nexport type DrillAuthorisation =\r\n  | { kind: "homework"; id: string }\r\n  | { kind: "guided"; id: string }\r\n  | { kind: "practice-test"; pt: number }\r\n  | { kind: "bootcamp" }\r\n  | null;\r\n\r\nexport function drillAuthorisation(\r\n  launch: DrillLaunch | null,\r\n  ptRunId: string | null,\r\n  ptOf: (runId: string) => number | null = ptOfRun as (runId: string) => number | null,\r\n  /** `?assignmentId=` from the oldest classroom links, which name no launch kind. */\r\n  legacyAssignmentId: string | null = null,\r\n): DrillAuthorisation {\r\n  if (ptRunId) {\r\n    const pt = ptOf(ptRunId);\r\n    if (pt !== null) return { kind: "practice-test", pt };\r\n  }\r\n  if (!launch) return legacyAssignmentId ? { kind: "homework", id: legacyAssignmentId } : null;\r\n  if (launch.kind === "questions" && launch.carry) {\r\n    const carry = launch.carry;\r\n    if (carry.ptLeg?.runId) {\r\n      const pt = ptOf(carry.ptLeg.runId);\r\n      if (pt !== null) return { kind: "practice-test", pt };\r\n    }\r\n    if (carry.guidedAssignmentId) return { kind: "guided", id: carry.guidedAssignmentId };\r\n    if (carry.assignmentId) return { kind: "homework", id: carry.assignmentId };\r\n  }\r\n  switch (launch.kind) {\r\n    case "assignment":\r\n      return { kind: "homework", id: launch.assignmentId };\r\n    case "guided-drill":\r\n      return launch.assignmentId ? { kind: "guided", id: launch.assignmentId } : null;\r\n    case "bootcamp-day":\r\n    case "practice-set":\r\n      return { kind: "bootcamp" };\r\n    case "section":\r\n      if (launch.ptLeg?.runId) {\r\n        const pt = ptOf(launch.ptLeg.runId);\r\n        if (pt !== null) return { kind: "practice-test", pt };\r\n      }\r\n      return null;\r\n    default:\r\n      return null;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function authoriseDrill(auth: NonNullable<DrillAuthorisation>, uid: string): Promise<boolean> {\r\n'}]

React Router keeps this component mounted\r\n    // across a search-param change, so without this the previous sitting's\r\n    // re-attempts, clock, scroll positions and open modal ride into the next one.\r\n    setError(null);\r\n    setSaveError(null);\r\n    setReattemptPicks(new Map());\r\n    setSkippedReattempt(new Set());\r\n    setFirstAttemptEliminations(new Map());\r\n    setScrollTops(new Map());\r\n    setElapsed(0);\r\n    setPassageOnly(false);\r\n    setFindQuery('');\r\n    setFindIndex(0);\r\n    setHighlightMode('none');\r\n    setModal(null);\r\n\r\n    (async () => {\r\n      try {\r\n        const existingId = params.get('sessionId');\r\n        const passageParam = params.get('passage');\r\n        const routed = (location.state ?? {}) as {\r\n          config?: RcDrillConfig;\r\n          passageIds?: number[];\r\n          sessionId?: string;\r\n        };\r\n\r\n        if (existingId) {\r\n          const session = await getSession(existingId);\r\n          if (!session) throw new Error('That drill could not be found.');\r\n          const loaded = await loadPassages(session.passageIds);\r\n          if (cancelled) return;\r\n          setConfig(session.config);\r\n          setElapsed(Math.max(session.config.clockElapsedSeconds ?? 0, readClockBackup(session.id)));\r\n          setSessionId(session.id);\r\n          setPassages(loaded);\r\n          return;\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        if (passageParam) {\r\n          const passage = await loadPassage(Number(passageParam));\r\n          if (!passage) throw new Error('That passage could not be found.');\r\n          const session = await createSession(DEFAULT_CONFIG, [passage.passageId]);\r\n          if (cancelled) return;\r\n          setConfig(DEFAULT_CONFIG);\r\n          setSessionId(session.id);\r\n          setPassages([passage]);\r\n          return;\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        if (routed.passageIds?.length) {\r\n          const nextConfig = routed.config ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG;\r\n          const loaded = await loadPassages(routed.passageIds);\r\n          const session =\r\n            routed.sessionId\r\n              ? await getSession(routed.sessionId)\r\n              : await createSession(nextConfig, routed.passageIds);\r\n          if (cancelled) return;\r\n          setConfig(session?.config ?? nextConfig);\r\n          if (session) {\r\n            setElapsed(Math.max(session.config.clockElapsedSeconds ?? 0, readClockBackup(session.id)));\r\n          }\r\n          setSessionId(session?.id ?? null);\r\n          setPassages(loaded);\r\n          return;\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        throw new Error('No drill to run. Start one from the Reading Comprehension hub.');\r\n      } catch (err) {\r\n        if (!cancelled) setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Something went wrong.');\r\n      }\r\n    })();\r\n\r\n    return () => {\r\n      cancelled = true;\r\n    };\r\n    // Re-boot when the drill being pointed at changes: React Router keeps this\r\n    // component mounted across a search-param change, so an empty dep list\r\n    // would leave a stale session on screen (resume, drill-your-misses).\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\r\n  }, [params.get('sessionId'), params.get('passage'), location.key]);\r\n\r\n  // Seed the machine once the passages land, and restore prior work on resume.\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!passages) return;\r\n    const seeded = initialState(passages);\r\n    const targetIndex = targetQuestionId\r\n      ? seeded.items.findIndex((item) => item.question.questionId === targetQuestionId)\r\n      : -1;\r\n    dispatch({\r\n      type: 'restore',\r\n      state: {\r\n        ...seeded,\r\n        currentIndex: targetIndex >= 0 ? targetIndex : seeded.currentIndex,\r\n      },\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    if (!sessionId) return;\r\n    // The component stays mounted across a session change, so this promise can\r\n    // land after the player has already moved to a different drill. Without the\r\n    // guard, session A's answers get restored into session B.\r\n    let cancelled = false;\r\n    getSessionAttempts(sessionId)\r\n      .then((attempts) => {\r\n        if (cancelled || attempts.length === 0) return;\r\n        const hydrated = hydrateFromAttempts({\r\n          items: seeded.items,\r\n          attempts,\r\n          revealMode: config.reveal,\r\n          reviewOnly,\r\n        });\r\n        dispatch({\r\n          type: 'restore',\r\n          state: {\r\n            answers: hydrated.answers,\r\n            // In review this stays empty: `state.eliminations` is what a\r\n            // re-attempt writes, and seeding it with the FIRST attempt's\r\n            // cross-outs would file them against the second. The restored set\r\n            // goes to `firstAttemptEliminations`, for display only. Resuming a\r\n            // live drill is the opposite case — there they are still the\r\n            // student's working set, so they come straight back.\r\n            eliminations: reviewOnly ? new Map() : hydrated.eliminations,\r\n            flagged: hydrated.flagged,\r\n            recorded: hydrated.recorded,\r\n            revealed: hydrated.revealed,\r\n            reviewedPassages: hydrated.reviewedPassages,\r\n            currentIndex:\r\n              targetIndex >= 0\r\n                ? targetIndex\r\n                : hydrated.firstUnansweredIndex === -1\r\n                  ? 0\r\n                  : hydrated.firstUnansweredIndex,\r\n            submitted: hydrated.submitted,\r\n          },\r\n        });\r\n        // What the student crossed out the first time is theirs to see in\r\n        // review. It is display only: the array written with a re-attempt is\r\n        // built from what they do on this visit, not from this.\r\n        setFirstAttemptEliminations(hydrated.eliminations);\r\n      })\r\n      .catch(() => {\r\n        /* resuming without prior answers is still a usable drill */\r\n      });\r\n\r\n    return () => {\r\n      cancelled = true;\r\n    };\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\r\n  }, [passages, sessionId, targetQuestionId]);\r\n\r\n  /* ----------------------------------------------------------- tools */\r\n\r\n  const a11y = useRcA11y();\r\n  const passageIds = React.useMemo(() => (passages ?? [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "\r\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------- clocks */\r\n\r\n  const [elapsed, setElapsed] = React.useState(0);\r\n  const elapsedRef = React.useRef(0);\r\n  elapsedRef.current = elapsed;\r\n  const questionStart = React.useRef<number>(Date.now());\r\n\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (state.paused || !sessionId || !passages || reviewOnly) return;\r\n    let lastTick = Date.now();\r\n    const id = window.setInterval(() => {\r\n      const now = Date.now();\r\n      const delta = Math.max(0, (now - lastTick) / 1000);\r\n      lastTick = now;\r\n      setElapsed((e) => {\r\n        const next = e + delta;\r\n        writeClockBackup(sessionId, next);\r\n        return next;\r\n      });\r\n    }, 1000);\r\n    return () => window.clearInterval(id);\r\n  }, [state.paused, sessionId, passages, reviewOnly]);\r\n\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!sessionId || reviewOnly) return;\r\n    const save = () => {\r\n      writeClockBackup(sessionId, elapsedRef.current);\r\n      void saveSessionClock(sessionId, elapsedRef.current).catch((err) => {\r\n        if (isHandoffError(err)) {\r\n          setError('This practice test is active on another device. This device has stopped saving.');\r\n          return;\r\n        }\r\n        setSaveError('The section clock did not save.');\r\n        console.error('[rc:clock] save failed', err);\r\n      });\r\n    };\r\n    const heartbeat = window.setInterval(save, 5000);\r\n    window.addEventListener('pagehide', save);\r\n    return () => {\r\n      window.clearInterval(heartbeat);\r\n      window.removeEventListener('pagehide', save);\r\n      save();\r\n    };\r\n  }, [sessionId, reviewOnly]);\r\n\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    questionStart.current = Date.now();\r\n  }, [state.currentIndex]);\r\n\r\n  const budget = totalBudgetSeconds(config, passages?.length ?? 0);\r\n  const clockSeconds = budget == null ? Math.floor(elapsed) : budget - Math.floor(elapsed);\r\n  const overBudget = budget != null && clockSeconds < 0;\r\n\r\n  /* --------------------------------------------------------- writing */\r\n\r\n  const writeAttempt = React.useCallback(\r\n    async (target: RcSessionItem, selected: RcLetter | null): Promise<boolean> => {\r\n      if (!sessionId) return false;\r\n      // A question already written once in this session is not written again.\r\n      // Resume used to lose the reveal state, which left Confirm enabled on a\r\n      // question that was already answered and inserted a second attempt row\r\n      // for it. The reveal state is restored now; this is the belt.\r\n      if (state.recorded.has(target.question.questionId)) return true;\r\n      const timeMs = Math.max(0, Date.now() - questionStart.current);\r\n      try {\r\n        await recordAttempt({\r\n          sessionId,\r\n          questionId: target.question.questionId,\r\n          passageId: target.passage.passageId,\r\n          selected,\r\n          isCorrect: selected ? selected === target.question.correctAnswer : null,\r\n"}]

## assistant

Wayfinder #160 is **decided but only partly implemented** on current `main`.

- Full PrepTests have account ownership, saved runs, revision checks, local backups, and explicit second-device takeover in `src/lib/practiceTestStore.ts`. Ownerless browser records are left untouched and excluded from account Attempts.
- Standalone LR sections save answers, position, and elapsed time locally, then sync to the account in the background. RC saves attempts and clock state.
- The approved failed-save behavior is **missing**: LR and RC can show a save error while the clock continues. Full PrepTest transition saves block the next section, but a failed save does not pause every exam clock.
- Access gates preserve completed results and restrict resuming after access changes. The database migrations exist, but this code audit does not prove they are deployed live.
- The dirty feature checkout is older than `main` and still has a browser-only full PrepTest runner. Implementation should start from fresh `main`.
